used) / beds * 100.0\n\n# stats H42:L47\nfor col_letter in col_letters:\n    xs = [computed[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}42\"] = min(xs)\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}43\"] = max(xs)\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}44\"] = float(median(xs))\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}45\"] = sum(xs) / len(xs)\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}46\"] = percentile_inc(xs, 0.25)\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}47\"] = percentile_inc(xs, 0.75)\n\n# weighted mean H50:L50\nfor col_letter in col_letters:\n    headrooms = [computed[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    weights = [computed[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(26, 32)]\n    num = sum(h * w for h, w in zip(headrooms, weights))\n    den = sum(weights)\n    if den == 0:\n        raise RuntimeError(f'Zero weight sum for {col_letter}')\n    computed[f\"{col_letter}50\"] = num / den\n\n# Sanity: every formula cell must have a computed value\nmissing = [ref for ref in k):\n    xs = sorted(values)\n    n = len(xs)\n    if n == 1:\n        return float(xs[0])\n    r = 1 + (n - 1) * k\n    lo = int(r)\n    hi = lo + 1\n    if hi > n:\n        return float(xs[-1])\n    frac = r - lo\n    return float(xs[lo - 1] + frac * (xs[hi - 1] - xs[lo - 1]))\n\n\ndef fmt_num(v):\n    if isinstance(v, bool):\n        return '1' if v else '0'\n    if isinstance(v, int):\n        return str(v)\n    if isinstance(v, float):\n        s = format(v, '.15g')\n        return '0' if s == '-0' else s\n    return str(v)\n\n# ---------- compute values ----------\nwb_data = for col in COL_LETTERS:\n        avail = computed[f\"{col}{r_open}\"]\n        used = computed[f\"{col}{r_used}\"]\n        beds = computed[f\"{col}{r_beds}\"]\n        computed[f\"{col}{r}\"] = (avail - used) / beds * 100.0\n\n# stats rows 42-47\nfor col in COL_LETTERS:\n    xs = [computed[f\"{col}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    computed[f\"{col}42\"] = min(xs)\n    computed[f\"{col}43\"] = max(xs)\n    computed[f\"{col}44\"] = float(median(xs))\n    computed[f\"{col}45\"] = sum(xs)/len(xs)\n    computed[f\"{col}46\"] = percentile_inc(xs, 0.25)\n    computed[f\"{col}47\"] = percentile_inc(xs, 0.75)\n\n# weighted mean row 50\nfor col in COL_LETTERS:\n    hs = [computed[f\"{col}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    ws = [computed[f\"{col}{r}\"] for r
